| R12-MESH-5035 | Latency-sensitive cross-region arbitrage is direct peer-to-peer | A direct node-to-node transport exists and is wired. qip-edge-node main builds a PeerSession when QIP_PEER_MESH_LISTEN and QIP_PEER_MESH_PEERS are set (apps/qip-edge-node/src/main.rs:416, :1092-1103). PeerLink owns a TCP listener and connections on its own thread, with 200 ms connect/write timeouts and backoff (apps/qip-edge-node/src/peer.rs:51-66, :206-290). Nothing routes through qip-fabricd. PeerSettings::read refuses any non-loopback address until ADR 0112's mTLS exists (peer.rs:28-35, :136-150), so no cross-region link can be configured. In production the only frames a node originates are its own EdgeState (PeerSession::turn, peer.rs:540-555). Token, Reserved, Fire and Fill have no non-test sender: grep finds no production caller of EpochDraft, reserve_own_legs, declare_fire or send_leg. node_limits leaves the clock offset unmeasured (peer.rs:479-489), so the endpoint refuses every inbound Token as clock_unknown (qip-contracts/src/peer.rs:629-645, :1004). | PARTIAL | (1) A cross-region (non-loopback, mTLS per ADR 0112) peer link. (2) A production originator of arbitrage coordination messages. (3) A measured clock offset so a node can join an epoch. (4) The verification's placement test, which would fail if peer messages were routed through qip-fabricd; no such test exists, and peer_mesh.rs proves only that the socket path works. |  | P1 | node-to-node test over the peer transport |  |

ReservationBook bounds amount and time (peer.rs:1312-1373). Neither has a production caller. ReservationBook is a free-standing grant, not the cell's RegionTable or capital ledger, and the gate is a caller-supplied closure, not the cell's risk gate. | PARTIAL | Integrate with the cell: reserve against the region's actual capital/inventory (RegionTable) under the cell's deterministic risk gate, from a production path. |  | P0 | cargo test -p qip-mesh --test peer_guarantees |  |

| R12-EVID-2031 | Every extracted claim becomes an Evidence object with full provenance | Every connector-admitted record gets an EvidenceRecord (source identity, timestamps, location, extraction method, authenticity signal, licence, confidence) built by evidence_of (connector/evidence.rs:150-196); EvidenceRecord::new refuses blank source, method, licence or statement (qip-evidence/src/evidence.rs:62-118); an event whose evidence cannot be formed or is on an impossible timeline is quarantined (connector/runtime.rs:918-970). But the record still enters the world model via platform.observe(batch.accepted) (fastbrain node.rs:138) independently of whether the world fabric admitted its evidence (refusals are only counted, evidence_intake.rs:140-147), the observed facts carry no link to their evidence id, and non-connector feeds (synthetic, replay, tape, live) forward no evidence at all (fastbrain feed.rs:580-588). | PARTIAL | Gate a record's entry into the world model on its evidence being admitted (or link each world-model fact to its evidence id), wrap claims from the live/tape feeds, and a test that fails when a claim reaches the world model without evidence. | R12-DATA-2012 | P0 | an integration test that a narrative poll produces EvidenceRecords |  |

The world fabric is now fed in production (evidence_intake.rs:103-150). Links are recorded on the new claim only: the earlier claim's corroborates/contradicts lists are not updated (fabric.rs:760-765 push onto `record`, the incoming one). The fabric is in-memory, bounded and evicts (fabric.rs:812-838); nothing journals it, and the intake module states durability is not claimed (evidence_intake.rs:33-38). | PARTIAL | Record the relation on both records, persist links to the event log, and a replay test proving links survive. | R12-EVID-2031 | P1 | cargo test -p qip-world-model evidence |  |

The world fabric is now fed by both brains (evidence_intake.rs:103). Nothing links a belief (NOW variable, thesis, world-model fact) into the graph, and sources_of/graph() have no production reader (grep for sources_of( and .graph() outside qip-evidence finds none in kernel/apps). | PARTIAL | Link beliefs to the claims they rest on and provide/test a walk from a belief to every source document hash. | R12-EVID-2032 | P1 | a test walking a belief back to source hashes |  |

Authenticity multiplies into belief_weight (fabric.rs:719-723). In production the connector attaches only HashVerified with hash = the document hash it was given (connector/evidence.rs:184-187), so the check compares the body hash with itself and is always credited (+0.3, score 0.5); the world fabric is built with EvidenceFabric::default() and no trusting_registry call (world.rs:108; trusting_registry has no caller), so registered-issuer credit cannot occur; belief_weight is read by nothing outside qip-evidence. | PARTIAL | Production signals that are independent of the fetch (TLS identity from the transport, a published issuer hash or signature, a trusted registry configured), signature/issuer verification, and the resulting weight feeding a belief. | R12-EVID-2031 | P1 | a test that authenticity changes evidence weight |  |
| R12-EVID-2035 | Corroboration discounts common-source dependence | admit does not count a same-origin claim (same statement fingerprint or document hash) as corroboration (fabric.rs:667-679), and independent_origins collapses copies by document hash or text (fabric.rs:595-622). The world fabric now spans sources in production (evidence_intake.rs:103). Neither independent_origins nor the fabric's corroboration/contests is read by any production path (grep for independent_origins and .contests() outside qip-evidence finds no caller); REASON does not weigh it. | PARTIAL | Have the decision path weigh corroboration by independent origins; detect near-duplicate (re-bylined) text rather than only exact fingerprints; a property test over generated copy sets. |  | P0 | cargo test -p qip-reasoning-engine --test reasoning |  |
| R12-EVID-2036 | Claims are checked for temporal consistency | check_temporal_consistency marks a claim dated after its publication, ingested before publication, preceding its declared cause, or contradicting earlier-known state (qip-evidence/src/evidence.rs:189-250); the fabric runs it on admit and holds an impossible-timeline claim at zero weight (fabric.rs:646-653), and the connector runtime quarantines it (connector/runtime.rs:957-962) on every connector poll from both brains. But production evidence never declares a cause: evidence_of sets claimed_at and location and never calls with_cause (connector/evidence.rs:170-196; grep for with_cause/caused_by outside qip-evidence finds no evidence caller), so the event-ordering arm cannot fire in production. | PARTIAL | Populate caused_by on production claims so the ordering check can fire, and a runtime-level test of the quarantine branch. | R12-EVID-2031 | P1 | cargo test -p qip-world-model --lib evidence |  |
| R12-EVID-2037 | Claims are checked for geospatial consistency | check_geographic_consistency records location/routing/weather/jurisdiction disagreement (evidence.rs:293-360) and the fabric runs it on admit (fabric.rs:647). On the production path every claim's location is its source manifest's region (connector/evidence.rs:183, `region:{:?}`), so two claims about one entity can never carry incompatible coordinates from the evidence itself. | PARTIAL | Extract claim locations from the content (or a geo source) so the check can detect a real disagreement in production. | R12-EVID-2031 | P2 | a unit test with contradictory locations |  |
| R12-EVID-2038 | Contradictions kept as competing hypotheses until resolved | The fabric keeps every hypothesis for a claim key and resolves a contest only when a later claim with authenticity >= RESOLVING_AUTHENTICITY (0.6) arrives, recording the resolving id and keeping losers (fabric.rs:56, :657-704). The world fabric is now fed across sources, so cross-source contests open in production (evidence_intake.rs:121-131). On production evidence a contest can never resolve: the only signal attached is the tautological HashVerified (connector/evidence.rs:184-187), giving 0.2+0.3=0.5 < 0.6, and no registry is trusted (world.rs:108). Hypotheses are dropped on capacity eviction (fabric.rs:812-826). Nothing downstream reads contests. | PARTIAL | Production evidence able to reach the resolving threshold (real authenticity signals), a consumer of contests, and contest retention that survives eviction/restart. | R12-EVID-2032 | P1 | cargo test -p qip-reasoning-engine contradiction |  |
| R12-EVID-2039 | Adversarial and poisoned sources are detected | detect_poisoning scans the world fabric for coordinated manipulation, source poisoning, spoofing, synthetic content and spam (world-model poisoning.rs:39-50); Platform::evidence_poisoning runs it and counts findings (evidence_intake.rs:157-175); the deep brain's research loop calls it every sweep and raises each finding as a data gap (research.rs:854-858), on by default (ResearchConfig::default every_cycles 1, research.rs:232-241; main.rs:718-750). The fabric it reads is now fed (fastbrain node.rs:137, deepbrain connectors.rs:365). On production evidence the Spoofing class cannot fire (the only signal is a self-matching hash, connector/evidence.rs:184-187) and SourcePoisoning depends on contest resolutions that cannot occur (see EVID-2038). | PARTIAL | Production evidence carrying independent authenticity signals so spoofing and source-poisoning are detectable; the coordinated-campaign path only fires where statement text matches exactly (connector statements embed the source id, connector/evidence.rs:157-168, so only News headlines can coincide). | R12-EVID-2031 | P0 | a test feeding a coordinated campaign through PoisoningDetector::analyse |  |
| R12-EVID-2040 | Quantum methods never confer authenticity or truth | No quantum work on evidence graphs exists; there is also no structural guard because there is no evidence graph. | MISSING | Nothing to assess until an evidence graph exists; a guard should accompany any quantum clustering work. | R12-EVID-2033 | P3 | inspection |  |
| R12-EVID-3081 | External evidence re-fetchable by manifest | Every BeliefDelta and NowVariable must cite at least one ManifestRef{source, reference} or is refused (now.rs:267-290, :653-657, :745-750). In the production NowStage the references name in-process sources: BAR_SOURCE "kernel/bar-history" with "subject@close_time" (now_stage.rs:61, :348), "cycle-N" for infrastructure/portfolio readings (now_stage.rs:487-507), and world-model feature keys (now_stage.rs:518-529). Nothing resolves a ManifestRef to a data-catalogue manifest entry or external source reference; ManifestRef::lineage only formats a string (now.rs:288). | PARTIAL | Cite real manifest entries (connector manifest/source id plus fetch digest) and a resolver from ManifestRef to its source reference, with a test that each delta's citation resolves. | R12-WORLD-3075 | P1 | new test to be written (see gap); cargo test -p <owning crate> |  |
| R12-EVID-7010 | Append-only object/event archive holds immutable evidence | modules/evidence exists in Terraform; event log is hash-chained; fabricd archives sealed segments. | PARTIAL | No end-to-end proof that ledger evidence lands in a retention-locked bucket from a deployed process. |  | P0 | terraform plan for module.evidence + archive integration test |  |

BundleVerifier refuses a signature under a key outside the bundle (trust.rs tests). qip-api picks the signer per class (policy, halt, envelope) at start-up and proves it against the bundle (apps/qip-api/src/signing.rs; main.rs:306). Edge nodes verify through qip-edge/src/trust.rs.
